   102	  try {
   103	    single = JSON.parse(text);
   104	    singleOk = true;
   105	  } catch {
   106	    singleOk = false;
   107	  }
   108	  if (singleOk) {
   109	    if (Array.isArray(single)) {
   110	      return single.every(isEventShaped) ? { ok: true, raw: { schema: 'baton/event@1', events: single } } : { ok: false };
   111	    }
   112	    // A closest-miss of the documented wrapper — an events array whose schema
   113	    // key is missing or wrong — must ERROR, never silently degrade to a junk
   114	    // note with ok:true (the model cannot self-correct from a success
   115	    // envelope while its narrative was discarded).
   116	    if (single !== null && typeof single === 'object' && Array.isArray(single.events) && single.schema !== 'baton/event@1') {
   117	      return { ok: false, why: `stdin carries an events array but schema is ${single.schema === undefined ? 'missing' : JSON.stringify(single.schema)} — use {"schema":"baton/event@1","events":[…]}` };
   118	    }
   119	    // A single bare event object (string `type`, none of the raw-hook payload
   120	    // markers) is one of the two most likely readings of "pipe events on
   121	    // stdin" — accept it as that event instead of degrading it to a junk note.
   122	    if (
   123	      isEventShaped(single) &&
   124	      single.schema === undefined &&
   125	      !('hook_event_name' in single) &&
   126	      !('session_id' in single) &&
   127	      !('event' in single)
   128	    ) {
   129	      return { ok: true, raw: { schema: 'baton/event@1', events: [single] } };
   130	    }
   131	    return { ok: true, raw: single };
   132	  }
   133	  const lines = text.split('\n').map((l) => l.trim()).filter((l) => l !== '');
   134	  if (lines.length === 0) return { ok: false };
   135	  /** @type {any[]} */
   136	  const events = [];
   137	  for (const line of lines) {
   138	    let v;
   139	    try {
   140	      v = JSON.parse(line);
   141	    } catch {
   142	      return { ok: false };
   143	    }
   144	    if (v && typeof v === 'object' && v.schema === 'baton/event@1' && Array.isArray(v.events)) {
   145	      if (!v.events.every(isEventShaped)) return { ok: false };
   146	      events.push(...v.events);
   147	    } else if (isEventShaped(v)) {
   148	      events.push(v);
   149	    } else {
   150	      return { ok: false };
   151	    }
   152	  }
   153	  return { ok: true, raw: { schema: 'baton/event@1', events } };
   154	}
   155	
   156	/**
   157	 * `baton checkpoint` — mechanical checkpoint from a hook payload on stdin.
   158	 * Hook-safety rule: exit 0 on every soft failure so a checkpoint can never
   159	 * break the host harness; `--strict` opts into hard failures (CI).
   160	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
   161	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
   162	 */
   163	export async function cmdCheckpoint(args, io) {
   164	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(args, { platform: 'string', model: 'string', trigger: 'string', debounce: 'string', strict: 'boolean', 'take-over': 'boolean' });
   165	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'checkpoint', parsed.error);
   166	  const flags = parsed.flags;
   167	  const strict = flags.strict === true;
   168	  const platform = typeof flags.platform === 'string' ? flags.platform : null;
   169	  if (!platform) return usageError(io, flags, 'checkpoint', '--platform <claude-code|codex|cursor> is required');
   170	  const pErr = platformError(platform);
   171	  if (pErr) return usageError(io, flags, 'checkpoint', pErr);
   172	
   173	  try {
   174	    return await run(flags, platform, io);
   175	  } catch (err) {
   176	    const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err);
   177	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'checkpoint-failed', msg } });
   178	    io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: ${msg}\n`);
   179	    return strict ? 1 : 0;
   180	  }
   181	}
   182	
   183	/**
   184	 * @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags
   185	 * @param {string} platform
   186	 * @param {any} io
   187	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
   188	 */
   189	async function run(flags, platform, io) {
   190	  const strict = flags.strict === true;
   191	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
   192	  const paths = bundlePaths(root);
   193	  // The --json contract (gate-2 fix 11): exactly one envelope on stdout for
   194	  // every exit path — the exit code stays governed by hook-safety.
   195	  const finish = (/** @type {any} */ env, /** @type {number} */ code) => {
   196	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, env);
   197	    return code;
   198	  };
   199	
   200	  const parsed = parseStdin(typeof io.stdin === 'string' ? io.stdin : '');
   201	  if (!parsed.ok) {
   202	    const why = parsed.why ?? 'send one JSON value ({"schema":"baton/event@1","events":[…]}), a JSON array of events, or NDJSON event lines';
   203	    io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: stdin could not be parsed — ${why}\n`);
   204	    return finish({ ok: false, error: { code: 'bad-stdin', msg: why } }, strict ? 1 : 0);
   205	  }
   206	  const raw = parsed.raw;
   207	
   208	  const session = { host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime };
   209	  // The hook command declares which event it is (--trigger), so event identity
   210	  // never depends on parsing each harness's payload shape (Cursor's stop payload
   211	  // names the event nowhere normalize could find it).
   212	  const explicitEvent = typeof flags.trigger === 'string' ? flags.trigger : undefined;
   213	  const events = normalizeHookPayload(raw, platform, session, explicitEvent);
   214	  if (events.length === 0) return finish({ ok: true, data: { events: 0, rewritten: false } }, 0);
   215	
   216	  // Opt-in transcript tail (gate-2 fix 10): rides the journal as its own
   217	  // IMPORTANT event so the snapshot rewrite below persists it, and purge can
   218	  // strip it from every retained copy by its property name.
   219	  const tail = captureTranscriptTail(root, raw, io);
   220	  if (tail !== null) {
   221	    events.push({
   222	      type: 'transcript.set',
   223	      payload: { transcript: { capturedAt: io.now(), tail } },
   224	      source: platform,
   225	      sessionHint: events[0]?.sessionHint,
   226	      unstable: events[0]?.unstable ?? true,
   227	    });
   228	  }
   229	
   230	  const { bundle, warnings, unsafe } = loadBundle(root, io);
   231	  for (const w of warnings) io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: ${w}\n`);
   232	
   233	  // An unsafe managed tree (symlinked .handoff — gate-2 fix 6) is NOT "no
   234	  // bundle yet": seeding here would write through the link. Refuse everything;
   235	  // hook-safety keeps the refusal soft outside --strict.
   236	  if (unsafe === true) {
   237	    io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: managed tree failed the symlink/realpath jail — nothing written\n');
   238	    return finish({ ok: false, error: { code: 'unsafe-tree', msg: warnings[0] ?? 'managed tree failed the jail check' } }, strict ? 1 : 0);
   239	  }
   240	
   241	  // Opt-in debounce (gate-2 major 14; Cursor afterFileEdit fires per edit):
   242	  // at most one real checkpoint per window, tracked by a per-platform stamp.
   243	  // The read-and-set runs UNDER the repo lock (gate-2 iter-2 M5) so two
   244	  // simultaneous afterFileEdit hooks can't both observe no stamp and both
   245	  // checkpoint — exactly one wins the window.
   246	  if (flags.debounce !== undefined) {
   247	    const seconds = Number(flags.debounce);
   248	    if (!Number.isFinite(seconds) || seconds <= 0) return usageError(io, flags, 'checkpoint', '--debounce <seconds> must be a positive number');
   249	    const stampPath = `${paths.logDir}/debounce-${platform}.json`;
   250	    const proceed = withLock(root, io, () => {
   251	      const stamp = safeReadJson(io.fs, stampPath);
   252	      if (stamp.ok && typeof stamp.value?.at === 'string' && Date.parse(io.now()) - Date.parse(stamp.value.at) < seconds * 1000) {
   253	        return false;
   254	      }
   255	      ensureDir(io.fs, paths.logDir);
   256	      atomicWriteJson(io.fs, stampPath, { at: io.now() });
   257	      return true;
   258	    });
   259	    if (!proceed) return finish({ ok: true, data: { events: 0, rewritten: false, debounced: true } }, 0);
   260	  }
   261	
   262	  let active = bundle;
   263	  let mustRewrite = false;
   264	
   265	  // Semantic isolation (plan §Concurrency): a stable foreign session or a
   266	  // mismatched origin platform must not merge into the active bundle. Checked
   267	  // before ANY write so a rejection leaves the whole tree untouched.
   268	  const originHint = active?.origin?.sessionHint;
   269	  const hasStableOwner = active !== null && typeof originHint === 'string' && originHint.length > 0 && active.origin.unstable !== true;
   270	  const stableIncoming = events.find(
   271	    (/** @type {any} */ e) => typeof e.sessionHint === 'string' && e.sessionHint.length > 0 && e.unstable === false,
   272	  );
   273	  const unstableIncoming = events.some((/** @type {any} */ e) => e.unstable === true);
   274	
   275	  if (hasStableOwner) {
   276	    // Foreign = a different stable session, OR any platform mismatch — the
   277	    // platform is provable even when the events carry no session hint (audit
   278	    // finding: a hint-less /baton:handoff narrative from claude-code silently
   279	    // merged into — then sealed — a codex-owned bundle, corrupting the origin
   280	    // the receive-side role avoidance keys on).
   281	    const incomingLabel = stableIncoming ? stableIncoming.sessionHint : '(no session hint)';
   282	    const foreign = stableIncoming
   283	      ? stableIncoming.sessionHint !== originHint || platform !== active.origin.platform
   284	      : platform !== active.origin.platform;
   285	    if (foreign && flags['take-over'] !== true) {
   286	      io.stderr.write(
   287	        `baton checkpoint: event from a foreign session/origin (${platform}/${incomingLabel} vs active ${active.origin.platform}/${originHint}) — rejected to keep sessions isolated; rerun with --take-over to archive the active bundle and start fresh\n`,
   288	      );
   289	      return finish({ ok: false, error: { code: 'foreign-session', msg: 'event from a foreign session/origin rejected; rerun with --take-over' } }, 0);
   290	    }
   291	    if (foreign) {
   292	      rotateJournal(root, 'takeover', io);
   293	      const fresh = emptyBundle(
   294	        { platform, model: typeof flags.model === 'string' ? flags.model : 'unknown', goal: active.task?.goal ?? 'unknown' },
   295	        io.now(),
   296	      );
   297	      if (stableIncoming) fresh.origin.sessionHint = stableIncoming.sessionHint;
   298	      writeSnapshot(root, fresh, io);
   299	      io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: took over — prior bundle archived to history/, fresh bundle owned by ${platform}/${incomingLabel}\n`);
   300	      active = fresh;
   301	      mustRewrite = true;
   302	    }
   303	  }
   304	
   305	  if (unstableIncoming) {
   306	    io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: event carries an unstable (generated) session hint — ownership unverifiable; applied with this warning only\n');
   307	  }
   308	
   309	  if (active === null) {
   310	    active = emptyBundle(
   311	      { platform, model: typeof flags.model === 'string' ? flags.model : 'unknown', goal: 'unknown' },
   312	      io.now(),
   313	    );
   314	    writeSnapshot(root, active, io);
   315	    // Self-applying seed event (gate-2 iter-2 B6): a journal-only rebuild
   316	    // (snapshot + .bak both lost) must restore the bundle's identity, not the
   317	    // "unknown" placeholder resolveBase seeds. bundle.seed carries bundleId,
   318	    // origin, task, and generation and is applied on replay.
   319	    appendJournal(
   320	      root,
   321	      {
   322	        ts: io.now(),
   323	        type: 'bundle.seed',
   324	        payload: {
   325	          bundleId: active.bundleId,
   326	          generation: active.generation,
   327	          createdAt: active.createdAt,
   328	          origin: active.origin,
   329	          task: { goal: active.task.goal },
   330	          text: `[seed] auto-seeded a new bundle on ${platform} (model ${active.origin.model})`,
   331	        },
   332	        writerId: [platform, io.pid, 'seed'].join('-'),
   333	        dedupeKey: dedupeKey({ ts: io.now(), type: 'bundle.seed', bundleId: active.bundleId }),
   334	      },
   335	      io,
   336	    );
   337	    io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: no bundle found — auto-seeded a new one at .handoff/bundle.json\n');
   338	    mustRewrite = true;
   339	  }
   340

     1	import { dirname } from 'node:path';
     2	import { planInit } from '../scaffold/plan.mjs';
     3	import { planHarnessInit } from '../scaffold/harness.mjs';
     4	import { atomicWriteText, ensureDir } from '../util/fsx.mjs';
     5	import { emitEnvelope, resolveRoot, parseFlagsStrict, usageError } from './shared.mjs';
     6	
     7	/**
     8	 * `baton init` — two-phase scaffolder. The plan phase (planInit) is read-only;
     9	 * `--dry-run` stops there. Apply writes each planned action atomically;
    10	 * refusals (malformed settings, corrupt markers) warn and leave their file
    11	 * untouched while the rest of the scaffold still lands.
    12	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
    13	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
    14	 */
    15	export async function cmdInit(args, io) {
    16	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(args, { codex: 'boolean', cursor: 'boolean', 'with-legacy-prompts': 'boolean', force: 'boolean', check: 'boolean', 'dry-run': 'boolean' });
    17	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'init', parsed.error);
    18	  const flags = parsed.flags;
    19	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
    20	
    21	  // The strict parser rejects stray tokens outright (a pasted `# comment` used
    22	  // to be consumed as --dry-run's "value", silently running a REAL init), so
    23	  // boolean flags here are only ever true | undefined.
    24	  const checkMode = flags.check === true;
    25	  const dryRun = flags['dry-run'] === true;
    26	
    27	  /** @type {ReturnType<typeof planInit>} */
    28	  let actions;
    29	  try {
    30	    const base = planInit(root, { force: flags.force === true }, io);
    31	    // Feed the harness writer the gitignore text AS THE BASE SCAFFOLD LEAVES IT
    32	    // (planned write, else current file) so its own ignore lines chain on top —
    33	    // two independent reads would make the later write clobber the earlier one.
    34	    const gi = base.find((a) => a.id === 'gitignore');
    35	    /** @type {string | null} */
    36	    let gitignoreBase = null;
    37	    if (gi?.op === 'write') {
    38	      gitignoreBase = /** @type {string} */ (gi.preview);
    39	    } else {
    40	      try {
    41	        gitignoreBase = io.fs.readFileSync(`${root}/.gitignore`, 'utf8');
    42	      } catch {
    43	        gitignoreBase = null;
    44	      }
    45	    }
    46	    actions = [
    47	      ...base,
    48	      ...planHarnessInit(
    49	        root,
    50	        { codex: flags.codex === true, cursor: flags.cursor === true, withLegacyPrompts: flags['with-legacy-prompts'] === true, gitignoreBase },
    51	        io,
    52	      ),
    53	    ];
    54	  } catch (err) {
    55	    const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err);
    56	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'plan-failed', msg } });
    57	    else io.stderr.write(`baton init: ${msg}\n`);
    58	    return 1;
    59	  }
    60	
    61	  const describe = (/** @type {any} */ a) =>
    62	    a.op === 'write' ? `write ${a.path}` : a.op === 'skip' ? `skip  ${a.path} (${a.note})` : `REFUSE ${a.path} — ${a.note}`;
    63	
    64	  // --check (gate-2 fix 9): the CI drift gate. Read-only — a scaffolded tree
    65	  // where init would change nothing passes; anything init would write or
    66	  // refuse is drift and fails the check.
    67	  if (checkMode) {
    68	    const drift = actions.filter((a) => a.op !== 'skip');
    69	    if (flags.json) {
    70	      emitEnvelope(io, {
    71	        ok: drift.length === 0,
    72	        data: { drift: drift.map(({ id, path, op, note }) => ({ id, path, op, note: note ?? null })) },
    73	      });
    74	    } else if (drift.length === 0) {
    75	      io.stdout.write('baton init --check: no drift — rendered files match template output\n');
    76	    } else {
    77	      io.stderr.write(`baton init --check: drift detected — init would write:\n${drift.map((a) => `  ${describe(a)}`).join('\n')}\n`);
    78	    }
    79	    return drift.length === 0 ? 0 : 1;
    80	  }
    81	
    82	  if (dryRun) {
    83	    if (flags.json) {
    84	      emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { dryRun: true, actions: actions.map(({ id, path, op, note }) => ({ id, path, op, note: note ?? null })) } });
    85	    } else {
    86	      io.stdout.write(`baton init plan (dry run — nothing written):\n${actions.map((a) => `  ${describe(a)}`).join('\n')}\n`);
    87	    }
    88	    return 0;
    89	  }
    90	
    91	  /** @type {string[]} */
    92	  const warnings = [];
    93	  for (const a of actions) {
    94	    if (a.op === 'refuse') {
    95	      warnings.push(a.note ?? `refused ${a.path}`);
    96	      continue;
    97	    }
    98	    if (a.op !== 'write') continue;
    99	    ensureDir(io.fs, dirname(a.path));
   100	    atomicWriteText(io.fs, a.path, /** @type {string} */ (a.preview));
   101	  }
   102	  for (const w of warnings) io.stderr.write(`baton init: ${w} — left untouched\n`);
   103	
   104	  if (flags.json) {
   105	    emitEnvelope(io, {
   106	      ok: true,
   107	      data: { actions: actions.map(({ id, path, op, note }) => ({ id, path, op, note: note ?? null })) },
   108	      warnings,
   109	    });
   110	  } else {
   111	    io.stdout.write(`baton init:\n${actions.map((a) => `  ${describe(a)}`).join('\n')}\n`);
   112	  }
   113	  return 0;
   114	}

     6	// Harness-surface writer for `baton init --codex` / `--cursor`. Read-only
     7	// planner (same Action shape as scaffold/plan.mjs); merges are add-only —
     8	// pre-existing user hook entries always survive as exact objects, with baton's
     9	// entries coexisting in the same arrays. config.toml and the notify shim are
    10	// NEVER touched; legacy ~/.codex/prompts are opt-in via withLegacyPrompts.
    11	
    12	const ADAPTERS_DIR = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..', '..', '..', 'adapters');
    13	// Absolute path to baton's entry script, resolved from this module's location so
    14	// it is correct for both a linked checkout and a packed npm install.
    15	const BATON_ENTRY = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..', '..', 'bin', 'baton.mjs');
    16	
    17	/**
    18	 * The absolute `"<node>" "<baton.mjs>"` invocation embedded into hook commands.
    19	 * Bare `baton` only resolves when nvm/volta shims are on PATH — true in a
    20	 * terminal, FALSE in a GUI-launched harness (Cursor/Codex desktop app), whose
    21	 * hooks then silently no-op. Absolute paths make the command PATH-independent.
    22	 * @param {any} io @returns {string}
    23	 */
    24	function batonInvocation(io) {
    25	  const node = io.execPath || process.execPath;
    26	  return `"${node}" "${BATON_ENTRY}"`;
    27	}
    28	
    29	/**
    30	 * Rewrite a single hook command string's bare-`baton` prefix to `inv`. Already
    31	 * absolute (or foreign) commands are returned unchanged, so re-rewriting is a
    32	 * no-op — the property idempotent re-init relies on.
    33	 * @param {string} s @param {string} inv @returns {string}
    34	 */
    35	function rewriteCommand(s, inv) {
    36	  if (typeof s !== 'string') return s;
    37	  // cmd.exe /c strips the FIRST and LAST quote when the command starts with a
    38	  // quote and contains more than one pair, so `cmd /c "node" "script" args`
    39	  // executes `node" "script...`. The documented remedy: wrap the whole command
    40	  // in an OUTER quote pair (audit finding).
    41	  if (s.startsWith('cmd /c baton ')) return `cmd /c "${inv} ${s.slice('cmd /c baton '.length)}"`;
    42	  if (s.startsWith('baton ')) return `${inv} ${s.slice('baton '.length)}`;
    43	  return s;
    44	}
    45	
    46	/**
    47	 * Deep-walk a parsed hooks manifest, rewriting every `command`/`commandWindows`
    48	 * string. Shape-agnostic: handles Codex's nested `hooks:[{hooks:[…]}]` and
    49	 * Cursor's flat `[{command}]` alike.
    50	 * @param {any} node @param {string} inv @returns {any}
    51	 */
    52	function rewriteInvocations(node, inv) {
    53	  if (Array.isArray(node)) return node.map((v) => rewriteInvocations(v, inv));
    54	  if (node && typeof node === 'object') {
    55	    /** @type {Record<string, any>} */
    56	    const out = {};
    57	    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(node)) {
    58	      out[k] = (k === 'command' || k === 'commandWindows') && typeof v === 'string' ? rewriteCommand(v, inv) : rewriteInvocations(v, inv);
    59	    }
    60	    return out;
    61	  }
    62	  return node;
    63	}
    64	
    65	/**
    66	 * A hook entry baton owns: it names baton AND carries a `--platform codex|cursor`
    67	 * flag (both the old bare-`baton` form and the new absolute form). User entries
    68	 * never match, so they always survive; baton's own prior entry is replaced (not
    69	 * duplicated) on re-init even when the embedded node path drifts across upgrades.
    70	 * @param {any} entry @returns {boolean}
    71	 */
    72	function looksBatonManaged(entry) {
    73	  const s = JSON.stringify(entry);
    74	  return /--platform\s+(?:codex|cursor)/.test(s) && /baton/.test(s);
    75	}

   102	/**
   103	 * Merge a hooks manifest: for every baton event, drop the existing
   104	 * baton-managed entries (so re-init REPLACES a stale one rather than appending a
   105	 * duplicate) and append the current template entries; user entries and foreign
   106	 * events survive untouched; non-hooks top-level keys (e.g. Cursor's version) are
   107	 * preserved. `changed` is a deep comparison so a byte-identical re-init is a
   108	 * clean no-op even though the merge rebuilt the arrays.
   109	 * @param {any} existing parsed existing manifest, or null
   110	 * @param {any} tpl parsed template manifest (invocations already rewritten)
   111	 * @returns {{merged: any, changed: boolean}}
   112	 */
   113	function mergeHooks(existing, tpl) {
   114	  const merged = structuredClone(existing ?? {});
   115	  for (const key of Object.keys(tpl)) {
   116	    if (key !== 'hooks' && !(key in merged)) merged[key] = structuredClone(tpl[key]);
   117	  }
   118	  if (typeof merged.hooks !== 'object' || merged.hooks === null) merged.hooks = {};
   119	  for (const [event, entries] of Object.entries(tpl.hooks ?? {})) {
   120	    const existingArr = Array.isArray(merged.hooks[event]) ? merged.hooks[event] : [];
   121	    const kept = existingArr.filter((/** @type {any} */ e) => !looksBatonManaged(e));
   122	    merged.hooks[event] = [...kept, .../** @type {any[]} */ (entries).map((e) => structuredClone(e))];
   123	  }
   124	  return { merged, changed: existing === null || !isDeepStrictEqual(merged, existing) };
   125	}
   126	
   127	/**
   128	 * Plan one hooks-manifest merge into an Action.
   129	 * @param {string} id @param {string} targetPath @param {string} tplPath @param {any} io
   130	 * @returns {import('./plan.mjs').Action}
   131	 */
   132	function hooksAction(id, targetPath, tplPath, io) {
   133	  const tplText = readOrNull(io, tplPath);
   134	  if (tplText === null) return { id, path: targetPath, op: 'refuse', note: `packaged template missing at ${tplPath} — broken install` };
   135	  const existingText = readOrNull(io, targetPath);
   136	  /** @type {any} */
   137	  let existing = null;
   138	  if (existingText !== null) {
   139	    try {
   140	      existing = JSON.parse(existingText);
   141	    } catch {
   142	      return { id, path: targetPath, op: 'refuse', note: `${targetPath} is not valid JSON — refusing to merge into a file baton cannot parse` };
   143	    }
   144	  }
   145	  const tpl = rewriteInvocations(JSON.parse(tplText), batonInvocation(io));
   146	  const { merged, changed } = mergeHooks(existing, tpl);
   147	  if (!changed) return { id, path: targetPath, op: 'skip', note: 'baton hooks already present — merge is a no-op' };
   148	  return { id, path: targetPath, op: 'write', preview: JSON.stringify(merged, null, 2) + '\n' };
   149	}
   150	
   151	/**
   152	 * Plan a plain file copy from a packaged template (write-if-different).
   153	 * @param {string} id @param {string} targetPath @param {string} tplPath @param {any} io
   154	 * @returns {import('./plan.mjs').Action}
   155	 */
   156	function copyAction(id, targetPath, tplPath, io) {
   157	  const tplText = readOrNull(io, tplPath);
   158	  if (tplText === null) return { id, path: targetPath, op: 'refuse', note: `packaged template missing at ${tplPath} — broken install` };
   159	  if (readOrNull(io, targetPath) === tplText) return { id, path: targetPath, op: 'skip', note: 'already current' };
   160	  return { id, path: targetPath, op: 'write', preview: tplText };
   161	}
   162	
   163	/**
   164	 * The plan phase of the harness scaffold. Gated: no flags, no actions.
   165	 * @param {string} cwd
   166	 * @param {{codex?: boolean, cursor?: boolean, withLegacyPrompts?: boolean, gitignoreBase?: string | null}} opts
   167	 *   gitignoreBase: the .gitignore text AFTER the base scaffold's own gitignore
   168	 *   action (cmdInit passes it) so the two writers chain instead of clobber.
   169	 * @param {any} io
   170	 * @returns {import('./plan.mjs').Action[]}
   171	 */
   172	export function planHarnessInit(cwd, opts, io) {
   173	  /** @type {import('./plan.mjs').Action[]} */
   174	  const actions = [];
   175	
   176	  if (opts.codex === true) {
   177	    actions.push(hooksAction('codex-hooks', `${cwd}/.codex/hooks.json`, join(ADAPTERS_DIR, 'codex', 'hooks.json'), io));
   178	    actions.push(
   179	      copyAction(
   180	        'codex-skill',
   181	        `${cwd}/.agents/skills/baton-handoff/SKILL.md`,
   182	        join(ADAPTERS_DIR, 'codex', '.agents', 'skills', 'baton-handoff', 'SKILL.md'),
   183	        io,
   184	      ),
   185	    );
   186	    if (opts.withLegacyPrompts === true) {
   187	      const target = `${io.env?.HOME ?? ''}/.codex/prompts/baton-handoff.md`;
   188	      if (readOrNull(io, target) !== LEGACY_PROMPT_BODY) {
   189	        actions.push({ id: 'codex-legacy-prompt', path: target, op: 'write', preview: LEGACY_PROMPT_BODY });
   190	      } else {
   191	        actions.push({ id: 'codex-legacy-prompt', path: target, op: 'skip', note: 'already current' });
   192	      }
   193	    }
   194	  }
   195	
   196	  if (opts.cursor === true) {
   197	    actions.push(hooksAction('cursor-hooks', `${cwd}/.cursor/hooks.json`, join(ADAPTERS_DIR, 'cursor', 'hooks.json'), io));
   198	    for (const [id, rel] of CURSOR_FILES) {
   199	      actions.push(copyAction(id, `${cwd}/.cursor/${rel}`, join(ADAPTERS_DIR, 'cursor', rel), io));
   200	    }
   201	  }
   202	
   203	  // The written hook manifests embed THIS machine's absolute node path, so they
   204	  // are machine-specific and must never be committed — ensure ignore lines for
   205	  // each adapter initialized. Chained from gitignoreBase (the base scaffold's
   206	  // planned text) so this write is a superset, never a clobber.
   207	  if (opts.codex === true || opts.cursor === true) {
   208	    let text = opts.gitignoreBase !== undefined ? opts.gitignoreBase : readOrNull(io, `${cwd}/.gitignore`);
   209	    let changed = false;
   210	    const patterns = [...(opts.codex === true ? ['.codex/hooks.json'] : []), ...(opts.cursor === true ? ['.cursor/hooks.json'] : [])];
   211	    for (const p of patterns) {
   212	      const r = ensureIgnoreLine(text, p);
   213	      text = r.text;
   214	      changed = changed || r.changed;
   215	    }
   216	    actions.push(
   217	      changed
   218	        ? { id: 'harness-gitignore', path: `${cwd}/.gitignore`, op: 'write', preview: /** @type {string} */ (text) }
   219	        : { id: 'harness-gitignore', path: `${cwd}/.gitignore`, op: 'skip', note: '.gitignore already covers the machine-specific hook manifests' },
   220	    );
   221	  }
   222	
   223	  return actions;
   224	}

 }
 
+/** Event-shaped: an object carrying a string `type`. */
+const isEventShaped = (/** @type {any} */ v) => v !== null && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v) && typeof v.type === 'string';
+
+/**
+ * Tolerant stdin parse (live /baton:handoff failure: a model sent one event
+ * per line and the whole narrative checkpoint silently failed). Accept the
+ * canonical single JSON value, a bare array of events, or NDJSON where every
+ * line is an event object or a {schema, events} wrapper. All-or-nothing: one
+ * non-event element fails the whole parse — never a partial apply.
+ * @param {string} text @returns {{ok: true, raw: any} | {ok: false, why?: string}}
+ */
+function parseStdin(text) {
+  let single;
+  let singleOk = false;
+  try {
+    single = JSON.parse(text);
+    singleOk = true;
+  } catch {
+    singleOk = false;
+  }
+  if (singleOk) {
+    if (Array.isArray(single)) {
+      return single.every(isEventShaped) ? { ok: true, raw: { schema: 'baton/event@1', events: single } } : { ok: false };
+    }
+    // A closest-miss of the documented wrapper — an events array whose schema
+    // key is missing or wrong — must ERROR, never silently degrade to a junk
+    // note with ok:true (the model cannot self-correct from a success
+    // envelope while its narrative was discarded).
+    if (single !== null && typeof single === 'object' && Array.isArray(single.events) && single.schema !== 'baton/event@1') {
+      return { ok: false, why: `stdin carries an events array but schema is ${single.schema === undefined ? 'missing' : JSON.stringify(single.schema)} — use {"schema":"baton/event@1","events":[…]}` };
+    }
+    // A single bare event object (string `type`, none of the raw-hook payload
+    // markers) is one of the two most likely readings of "pipe events on
+    // stdin" — accept it as that event instead of degrading it to a junk note.
+    if (
+      isEventShaped(single) &&
+      single.schema === undefined &&
+      !('hook_event_name' in single) &&
+      !('session_id' in single) &&
+      !('event' in single)
+    ) {
+      return { ok: true, raw: { schema: 'baton/event@1', events: [single] } };
+    }
+    return { ok: true, raw: single };
+  }
+  const lines = text.split('\n').map((l) => l.trim()).filter((l) => l !== '');
+  if (lines.length === 0) return { ok: false };
+  /** @type {any[]} */
+  const events = [];
+  for (const line of lines) {
+    let v;
+    try {
+      v = JSON.parse(line);
+    } catch {
+      return { ok: false };
+    }
+    if (v && typeof v === 'object' && v.schema === 'baton/event@1' && Array.isArray(v.events)) {
+      if (!v.events.every(isEventShaped)) return { ok: false };
+      events.push(...v.events);
+    } else if (isEventShaped(v)) {
+      events.push(v);
+    } else {
+      return { ok: false };
+    }
+  }
+  return { ok: true, raw: { schema: 'baton/event@1', events } };
+}

+/** The supported platform enum, validated wherever a platform id is intake. */
+export const PLATFORMS = ['claude-code', 'codex', 'cursor'];
+
+/**
+ * Validate a platform id against the enum (surface-audit fold): a typo'd
+ * platform used to silently seed persistent state whose bogus origin then
+ * rejected the REAL platform's checkpoints as foreign.
+ * @param {string | boolean | undefined} value
+ * @returns {string | null} an error message, or null when valid/absent
+ */
+export function platformError(value) {
+  if (value === undefined) return null;
+  if (typeof value === 'string' && PLATFORMS.includes(value)) return null;
+  return `platform must be one of ${PLATFORMS.join('|')} (got ${JSON.stringify(value)})`;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Strict flag parser (surface-audit fold; docs/design/core.md §tooling always
+ * specified strict parsing). spec maps each flag name to 'string' | 'boolean';
+ * `json` and `root` are implied common flags. Errors — unknown flag, stray
+ * positional, missing string-flag value — return {error} so the command exits
+ * 2 instead of silently losing intent: the lenient parser let a stray token
+ * flip SAFETY flags off (checkpoint/doctor --strict, remap --native-only,
+ * receive --print-prompt all read fail-dangerous exit 0).
+ * @param {string[]} args
+ * @param {Record<string, 'string' | 'boolean'>} spec
+ * @returns {{flags: Record<string, string | boolean>, error?: string}}
+ */
+export function parseFlagsStrict(args, spec) {
+  /** @type {Record<string, 'string' | 'boolean'>} */
+  const full = { json: 'boolean', root: 'string', ...spec };
+  /** @type {Record<string, string | boolean>} */
+  const flags = {};
+  for (let i = 0; i < args.length; i += 1) {
+    const a = args[i];
+    if (!a.startsWith('--')) return { flags, error: `unexpected argument '${a}' — flags are --name [value]` };
+    const key = a.slice(2);
+    const kind = full[key];
+    if (kind === undefined) return { flags, error: `unknown flag --${key}` };
+    if (kind === 'boolean') {
+      // A following token that is not a flag would have been this flag's
+      // silent "value" under the lenient parser; reject it loudly.
+      const next = args[i + 1];
+      if (next !== undefined && !next.startsWith('--')) {
+        return { flags, error: `unexpected token '${next}' after --${key} (a bare flag takes no value)` };
+      }
+      flags[key] = true;
+      continue;
+    }
+    const next = args[i + 1];
+    // A string flag consumes the next token unless it is itself a KNOWN flag
+    // (so `--commit --origin x` errors instead of eating '--origin').
+    if (next === undefined || (next.startsWith('--') && full[next.slice(2)] !== undefined)) {
+      return { flags, error: `--${key} requires a value` };
+    }
+    flags[key] = next;
+    i += 1;
+  }
+  return { flags };
+}

+// Absolute path to baton's entry script, resolved from this module's location so
+// it is correct for both a linked checkout and a packed npm install.
+const BATON_ENTRY = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..', '..', 'bin', 'baton.mjs');
+
+/**
+ * The absolute `"<node>" "<baton.mjs>"` invocation embedded into hook commands.
+ * Bare `baton` only resolves when nvm/volta shims are on PATH — true in a
+ * terminal, FALSE in a GUI-launched harness (Cursor/Codex desktop app), whose
+ * hooks then silently no-op. Absolute paths make the command PATH-independent.
+ * @param {any} io @returns {string}
+ */
+function batonInvocation(io) {
+  const node = io.execPath || process.execPath;
+  return `"${node}" "${BATON_ENTRY}"`;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Rewrite a single hook command string's bare-`baton` prefix to `inv`. Already
+ * absolute (or foreign) commands are returned unchanged, so re-rewriting is a
+ * no-op — the property idempotent re-init relies on.
+ * @param {string} s @param {string} inv @returns {string}
+ */
+function rewriteCommand(s, inv) {
+  if (typeof s !== 'string') return s;
+  // cmd.exe /c strips the FIRST and LAST quote when the command starts with a
+  // quote and contains more than one pair, so `cmd /c "node" "script" args`
+  // executes `node" "script...`. The documented remedy: wrap the whole command
+  // in an OUTER quote pair (audit finding).
+  if (s.startsWith('cmd /c baton ')) return `cmd /c "${inv} ${s.slice('cmd /c baton '.length)}"`;
+  if (s.startsWith('baton ')) return `${inv} ${s.slice('baton '.length)}`;
+  return s;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Deep-walk a parsed hooks manifest, rewriting every `command`/`commandWindows`
+ * string. Shape-agnostic: handles Codex's nested `hooks:[{hooks:[…]}]` and
+ * Cursor's flat `[{command}]` alike.
+ * @param {any} node @param {string} inv @returns {any}
+ */
+function rewriteInvocations(node, inv) {
+  if (Array.isArray(node)) return node.map((v) => rewriteInvocations(v, inv));
+  if (node && typeof node === 'object') {
+    /** @type {Record<string, any>} */
+    const out = {};
+    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(node)) {
+      out[k] = (k === 'command' || k === 'commandWindows') && typeof v === 'string' ? rewriteCommand(v, inv) : rewriteInvocations(v, inv);
+    }
+    return out;
+  }
+  return node;
+}
+
+/**
+ * A hook entry baton owns: it names baton AND carries a `--platform codex|cursor`
+ * flag (both the old bare-`baton` form and the new absolute form). User entries
+ * never match, so they always survive; baton's own prior entry is replaced (not
+ * duplicated) on re-init even when the embedded node path drifts across upgrades.
+ * @param {any} entry @returns {boolean}
+ */
+function looksBatonManaged(entry) {
+  const s = JSON.stringify(entry);
+  return /--platform\s+(?:codex|cursor)/.test(s) && /baton/.test(s);
+}

    12	/**
    13	 * `baton detect` — classify harness output / structured error types.
    14	 * Exit codes ARE the verdict (frozen contract): 0 ok · 10 usage-limit ·
    15	 * 11 throttle · 12 auth · 13 other-error · 2 usage error.
    16	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
    17	 * @returns {number}
    18	 */
    19	export function cmdDetect(args, io) {
    20	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(args, { platform: 'string', text: 'string', 'exit-code': 'string', 'structured-error-type': 'string', signatures: 'string', explain: 'boolean' });
    21	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'detect', parsed.error);
    22	  const flags = parsed.flags;
    23	  const platform = typeof flags.platform === 'string' ? flags.platform : null;
    24	  if (!platform) return usageError(io, flags, 'detect', '--platform <claude-code|codex|cursor> is required');
    25	
    26	  let table;
    27	  try {
    28	    table = loadSignatures(
    29	      {
    30	        builtinPath: BUILTIN_SIGNATURES,
    31	        overlayPath: typeof flags.signatures === 'string' ? flags.signatures : undefined,
    32	      },
    33	      io,
    34	    );
    35	  } catch (err) {
    36	    const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err);
    37	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'bad-signatures', msg } });
    38	    else io.stderr.write(`baton detect: ${msg}\n`);
    39	    return 1;
    40	  }
    41	
    42	  const text = typeof flags.text === 'string' ? flags.text : typeof io.stdin === 'string' ? io.stdin : '';
    43	  const exitCode = flags['exit-code'] !== undefined ? Number(flags['exit-code']) : 0;
    44	
    45	  /** @type {{kind: string, errorType: string} | null} */
    46	  let structured = null;
    47	  if (typeof flags['structured-error-type'] === 'string') {
    48	    structured = {
    49	      kind: platform === 'codex' ? 'exec-json' : 'stop-failure',
    50	      errorType: flags['structured-error-type'],
    51	    };
    52	  }
    53	
    54	  const verdict = classify({ text, exitCode, platform, table, structured });
    55	  const data = { ...verdict, tableVersion: table.schema, tableUpdated: table.updated };
    56	  if (flags.explain) {
    57	    data.tried = table.signatures.filter((/** @type {any} */ s) => s.platform === platform).map((/** @type {any} */ s) => s.id);
    58	  }
    59	
    60	  if (flags.json) {
    61	    emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data });
    62	  } else {
    63	    io.stdout.write(`${verdict.class}${verdict.signatureId ? ` (${verdict.signatureId}, ${verdict.confidence})` : ''}${verdict.resetHint ? ` — resets ${verdict.resetHint}` : ''}\n`);
    64	  }
    65	  return EXIT_BY_CLASS[verdict.class] ?? 13;

    90	 * lock so a live foreign session blocks it entirely.
    91	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
    92	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
    93	 */
    94	export async function cmdPurgeTranscript(args, io) {
    95	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(args, {});
    96	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'purge-transcript', parsed.error);
    97	  const flags = parsed.flags;
    98	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
    99	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
   100	  const markerPath = `${p.dir}/purge.marker.json`;
   101	
   102	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.dir)) {
   103	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { purged: 0, note: 'no .handoff directory — nothing to purge' } });
   104	    else io.stdout.write('purge-transcript: no .handoff directory — nothing to purge\n');
   105	    return 0;
   106	  }
   107	
   108	  // Jail check BEFORE the lock: acquiring the lock mkdirs inside .handoff, so
   109	  // a symlinked tree must be refused before any write lands through the link.
   110	  const safe = checkHandoffTree(root, io);
   111	  if (!safe.ok) {
   112	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'unsafe-tree', msg: safe.problem } });
   113	    else io.stderr.write(`baton purge-transcript: ${safe.problem}\n`);
   114	    return 1;
   115	  }
   116	
   117	  // Auto-resume (gate-2 minor 16): a leftover marker means an earlier purge
   118	  // was interrupted mid-scrub — this run finishes the job.
   119	  if (io.fs.existsSync(markerPath)) {
   120	    io.stderr.write('baton purge-transcript: resuming an interrupted purge (marker found) — re-scrubbing the whole tree\n');
   121	  }
   122	
   123	  try {
   124	    withLock(root, io, () => {
   125	      atomicWriteJson(io.fs, markerPath, { startedAt: io.now() });
   126	      for (const file of walk(io, p.dir)) {
   127	        if (file === markerPath) continue;
   128	        if (/\.(ndjson|jsonl)$/.test(file)) scrubLinesFile(io, file);
   129	        else if (/\.(json|bak)$/.test(file)) scrubJsonFile(io, file);
   130	      }
   131	      io.fs.unlinkSync(markerPath);
   132	    });
   133	  } catch (err) {
   134	    const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err);
   135	    const code = err instanceof LockHeldError ? 'lock-held' : 'purge-failed';
   136	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code, msg } });
   137	    else io.stderr.write(`baton purge-transcript: ${msg}\n`);
   138	    return 1;
   139	  }
   140	
   141	  if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { note: 'transcript fields removed from every retained copy under .handoff/' } });
   142	  else io.stdout.write('purge-transcript: transcript fields removed from every retained copy under .handoff/\n');
   143	  return 0;
   144	}

     2	import { emitEnvelope, resolveRoot, parseFlagsStrict, usageError } from './shared.mjs';
     3	
     4	/**
     5	 * `baton recover` — explicit lock recovery (gate-2 fix 11: this sat in the
     6	 * frozen command list unimplemented). Exactly ONE state recovers: a
     7	 * provably-dead same-host owner. `--force` is an intent flag only — live,
     8	 * cross-host, and torn-metadata locks refuse with the same reasons either way
     9	 * (plan §Concurrency: never steal on uncertainty).
    10	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
    11	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
    12	 */
    13	export async function cmdRecover(args, io) {
    14	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(args, { force: 'boolean' });
    15	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'recover', parsed.error);
    16	  const flags = parsed.flags;
    17	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
    18	  const r = recoverLock(root, io, { force: flags.force === true });
    19	
    20	  if (r.recovered) {
    21	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { recovered: true } });
    22	    else io.stdout.write('baton recover: lock recovered (or already free) — nothing holds .handoff/lock\n');
    23	    return 0;
    24	  }
    25	  if (flags.json) {
    26	    emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, data: { recovered: false }, error: { code: 'refused', msg: r.refusedReason ?? 'refused' } });
    27	  } else {
    28	    io.stderr.write(`baton recover: ${r.refusedReason}\n`);
    29	  }
    30	  return 1;
    31	}
     1	import { loadConfig } from '../roles/matrix.mjs';
     2	import { resolveRoles } from '../roles/resolve.mjs';
     3	import { readProbeCache } from '../roles/availability.mjs';
     4	import { emitEnvelope, resolveRoot, usageError, parseFlagsStrict, platformError } from './shared.mjs';
     5	
     6	/**
     7	 * `baton remap` — resolve the committed role matrix for a destination platform.
     8	 * Read-only: prints assignments, never mutates the bundle (receive owns that).
     9	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
    10	 * @returns {number}
    11	 */
    12	export function cmdRemap(args, io) {
    13	  const parsed = parseFlagsStrict(args, { to: 'string', avoid: 'string', 'native-only': 'boolean' });
    14	  if (parsed.error !== undefined) return usageError(io, parsed.flags, 'remap', parsed.error);
    15	  const flags = parsed.flags;
    16	  const to = typeof flags.to === 'string' ? flags.to : null;
    17	  if (!to) return usageError(io, flags, 'remap', '--to <claude-code|codex|cursor> is required');
    18	  const pErr = platformError(to);
    19	  if (pErr) return usageError(io, flags, 'remap', pErr);
    20	
    21	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
    22	  const { config, errors } = loadConfig(root, io);
    23	  if (!config) {
    24	    const msg = errors.map((e) => (e.path ? `${e.path}: ${e.msg}` : e.msg)).join('; ');
    25	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'bad-config', msg } });
    26	    else io.stderr.write(`baton remap: ${msg}\n`);
    27	    return 1;
    28	  }
    29	
    30	  const avoid = typeof flags.avoid === 'string' ? flags.avoid.split(',') : [];
    31	  const { assignments, notes } = resolveRoles({
    32	    config,
    33	    to,
    34	    avoid,
    35	    nativeOnly: flags['native-only'] === true,
    36	    // The fresh doctor probe cache feeds eligibility (gate-2 major 13):
    37	    // rate-limited platforms are skipped like avoid[] entries; a stale or
    38	    // absent cache resolves offline with degraded flags.
    39	    probes: readProbeCache(root, io),
    40	  });
    41	
    42	  if (flags.json) {
    43	    emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { assignments, notes } });
    44	  } else {
    45	    for (const [role, a] of Object.entries(assignments)) {
    46	      io.stdout.write(`${role}: ${a.platform ?? '—'}/${a.model ?? '—'}${a.effort ? `@${a.effort}` : ''} (${a.mode})\n`);
    47	    }
    48	  }
    49	  return 0;
    50	}

   113	/**
   114	 * Load the active bundle with full crash recovery. Read path — not lock-gated;
   115	 * rotation-marker reconciliation is part of load. A managed tree that fails
   116	 * the symlink/realpath jail (gate-2 fix 6) is refused outright: bundle null,
   117	 * `unsafe: true`, and the problem in warnings — callers must not treat this as
   118	 * "no bundle yet" and seed through the link.
   119	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
   120	 * @returns {{bundle: any | null, warnings: string[], unsafe?: boolean}}
   121	 */
   122	export function loadBundle(root, io) {
   123	  const p = bundlePaths(root);
   124	  /** @type {string[]} */
   125	  const warnings = [];
   126	  if (!io.fs.existsSync(p.dir)) return { bundle: null, warnings };
   127	
   128	  const safe = checkHandoffTree(root, io);
   129	  if (!safe.ok) return { bundle: null, warnings: [safe.problem], unsafe: true };
   130	
   131	  reconcileMarker(io, p, warnings, root);
   132	
   133	  // An interrupted purge is visible on every read path (gate-2 minor 16): the
   134	  // tree may still hold transcript copies until purge-transcript finishes.
   135	  if (io.fs.existsSync(`${p.dir}/purge.marker.json`)) {
   136	    warnings.push('an interrupted purge left its marker — run baton purge-transcript to finish scrubbing every retained copy');
   137	  }
   138	
   139	  let bundle = resolveBase(io, p, warnings);
   140	  if (bundle === null) return { bundle: null, warnings };
   141	
   142	  if (io.fs.existsSync(p.journal)) {
   143	    const { entries, warnings: jw } = readAllTolerant(io.fs, p.journal);
   144	    for (const w of jw) {
   145	      warnings.push(`journal line ${w.line}: ${w.kind}${w.kind === 'seq-order' ? ` (prev ${w.prev}, got ${w.seq})` : ''}`);
   146	    }
   147	    for (const e of entries) {
   148	      // Envelope guard (gate-2 iter-2 B4): a parseable but non-object line
   149	      // (null, number, bare string) is not an event — skip it with a warning
   150	      // rather than crash replay on `e.seq` / `e.dedupeKey`.

    43	 * Verify the managed .handoff tree is safe to operate on: the directory itself
    44	 * is not a symlink, its realpath stays under the repository root, and no entry
    45	 * anywhere in the tree is a symlink (lstat walk — never follows links). An
    46	 * absent tree is safe; an unverifiable tree is refused, never trusted.
    47	 * @param {string} root @param {any} io
    48	 * @returns {{ok: true} | {ok: false, problem: string}}
    49	 */
    50	export function checkHandoffTree(root, io) {
    51	  const dir = `${root}/.handoff`;
    52	  try {
    53	    if (!io.fs.existsSync(dir)) return { ok: true };
    54	    if (io.fs.lstatSync(dir).isSymbolicLink()) {
    55	      return { ok: false, problem: `.handoff is a symlink — refusing to operate through a linked managed tree` };
    56	    }
    57	    // Compare on a canonical forward-slash spelling (Windows: realpathSync
    58	    // returns backslash paths; a drive-root repo would also yield a doubled
    59	    // `C://.handoff`). joinNorm collapses both so containment is platform-neutral
    60	    // and drive-root-safe (iter-3 F6).
    61	    const expected = joinNorm(io.fs.realpathSync(root), '.handoff');
    62	    const dirReal = normSep(io.fs.realpathSync(dir));
    63	    if (dirReal !== expected) {
    64	      return { ok: false, problem: `.handoff resolves outside the repository root (${dirReal}) — refusing` };
    65	    }
    66	    // Walk is race-tolerant (gate-2 iter-2): under concurrent atomic writes
    67	    // the tree churns — tmp files, the lock dir, and rotated journals appear
    68	    // and vanish between readdir and lstat. An entry that disappears mid-walk
    69	    // (ENOENT) is normal concurrency, NOT a symlink attack, so it is skipped;
    70	    // only a POSITIVELY observed symlink refuses. The stable top-level checks
    71	    // above (.handoff itself a symlink / realpath escape) stay strict.
    72	    /** @type {string[]} */
    73	    const stack = [dir];
    74	    while (stack.length > 0) {
    75	      const d = /** @type {string} */ (stack.pop());
    76	      /** @type {string[]} */
    77	      let names;
    78	      try {
    79	        names = io.fs.readdirSync(d);
    80	      } catch (e) {
    81	        if (/** @type {any} */ (e)?.code === 'ENOENT') continue; // subdir removed mid-walk
    82	        throw e;
    83	      }
    84	      for (const name of names) {
    85	        const p = `${d}/${name}`;
    86	        let st;
    87	        try {
    88	          st = io.fs.lstatSync(p);
    89	        } catch (e) {
    90	          if (/** @type {any} */ (e)?.code === 'ENOENT') continue; // entry vanished mid-walk
    91	          throw e;
    92	        }
    93	        if (st.isSymbolicLink()) return { ok: false, problem: `${p} is a symlink — refusing (managed-tree jail)` };
    94	        if (st.isDirectory()) stack.push(p);
    95	      }
    96	    }
    97	    return { ok: true };
    98	  } catch (err) {
    99	    const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err);
   100	    return { ok: false, problem: `managed tree could not be verified (${msg}) — refusing` };

    10	// Surface-audit fold: the lenient hand-rolled parser silently ate stray tokens
    11	// (a boolean flag's "value") and unknown flags, flipping SAFETY-RELEVANT flags
    12	// off with exit 0 — checkpoint --strict, doctor --strict, remap --native-only,
    13	// receive --print-prompt all fail-dangerous. docs/design/core.md §tooling
    14	// already promised strict parsing. parseFlagsStrict(args, spec) enforces it:
    15	// unknown flag, stray positional, or a missing string-flag value each return
    16	// {error} for a usage-error exit 2. --platform/--to values are validated

    26	    assert.equal(r.error, undefined);
    27	    assert.equal(r.flags.platform, 'codex');

    29	  });
    30

     1	const PRECEDENCE = ['usage-limit', 'auth', 'throttle', 'other-error'];
     2	
     3	/** @type {Record<string, string>} */
     4	const STOP_FAILURE_MAP = {
     5	  rate_limit: 'usage-limit',
     6	  overloaded: 'throttle',
     7	  authentication_failed: 'auth',
     8	  oauth_org_not_allowed: 'auth',
     9	  billing_error: 'auth',
    10	};
    11	
    12	// eslint-disable-next-line no-control-regex
    13	const ANSI = /\x1b\[[0-9;]*[A-Za-z]/g;
    14	
    15	/** @param {any} matcher @param {string} text */
    16	function matches(matcher, text) {
    17	  switch (matcher.kind) {
    18	    case 'substring':
    19	      return text.includes(matcher.value);
    20	    case 'regex':
    21	      return new RegExp(matcher.pattern, matcher.flags || '').test(text);
    22	    case 'json-field': {
    23	      for (const line of text.split('\n')) {
    24	        let obj;
    25	        try {
    26	          obj = JSON.parse(line);
    27	        } catch {
    28	          continue;
    29	        }
    30	        const val = String(matcher.path)
    31	          .split('.')
    32	          .reduce((o, k) => (o == null ? undefined : o[k]), obj);
    33	        if (val === matcher.equals) return true;
    34	      }
    35	      return false;
    36	    }
    37	    default:
    38	      return false;
    39	  }
    40	}
    41	
    42	/**
    43	 * Classify harness output. Structured evidence first (text and exit code are
    44	 * ignored when a recognized structured signal is present); text signatures as
    45	 * the fallback tier; exit code only breaks the no-match tie — a generic nonzero
    46	 * exit is never usage-limit on its own.
    47	 * @param {{text?: string, exitCode?: number, platform: string, table: any, structured?: {kind: string, errorType: string} | null}} input
    48	 * @returns {{class: string, signatureId: string | null, confidence: string | null, resetHint: string | null, tried?: string[]}}
    49	 */
    50	export function classify({ text = '', exitCode = 0, platform, table, structured = null }) {
    51	  if (structured) {
    52	    if (structured.kind === 'stop-failure') {
    53	      const cls = STOP_FAILURE_MAP[structured.errorType] ?? 'other-error';
    54	      return { class: cls, signatureId: `structured:${structured.errorType}`, confidence: 'high', resetHint: null };
    55	    }
    56	    if (structured.kind === 'exec-json') {
    57	      const cls = structured.errorType === 'usage_limit' ? 'usage-limit' : 'other-error';
    58	      return { class: cls, signatureId: `structured:${structured.errorType}`, confidence: 'high', resetHint: null };
    59	    }
    60	    // Unrecognized structured kind: fall through to the text tier.
    61	  }
    62	
    63	  const stripped = text.replace(ANSI, '');
    64	  const candidates = table.signatures.filter((/** @type {any} */ s) => s.platform === platform);
    65	  /** @type {any} */
    66	  let best = null;
    67	  for (const s of candidates) {
    68	    if (!matches(s.matcher, stripped)) continue;
    69	    if (best === null || PRECEDENCE.indexOf(s.class) < PRECEDENCE.indexOf(best.class)) best = s;
    70	  }
    71	
    72	  if (best) {
    73	    let resetHint = null;
    74	    if (typeof best.resetHint === 'string') {
    75	      const m = stripped.match(new RegExp(best.resetHint));
    76	      if (m) resetHint = m[1] ?? m[0];
    77	    }
    78	    return { class: best.class, signatureId: best.id, confidence: best.confidence, resetHint };
    79	  }
    80	
    81	  return {
    82	    class: exitCode !== 0 ? 'other-error' : 'ok',
    83	    signatureId: null,
    84	    confidence: null,
    85	    resetHint: null,
    86	  };
    87	}
